TL;DR: 本文提出基于中国计算机等级考试（NCRE）的办公自动化评测基准，含200个Word/Excel/PowerPoint实操任务，共7,118个机器可评分标准。测试了7个前沿LLM，单轮模型最高得分为36.6%，增强型智能体系统（含执行反馈、迭代修复）达68.8%，仍低于95.5%的社区参考分，表明当前代码生成LLM在精细办公自动化方面仍面临重大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在专业级生产力软件（如Office）中的自动化能力尚未得到充分测试。办公自动化需要长程规划推理、精确参数配置和多应用集成，是衡量文档自动化能力的理想环境。

Method: 基于中国NCRE考试构建评测基准，包含200个综合性实操任务（Word/Excel/PowerPoint）。每个任务按100分制评分，使用7,118条机器可评分标准。评测7个前沿LLM，包括单轮模型和增强型智能体系统（带执行反馈、迭代修复和更广泛的办公自动化接口）。

Result: 单轮LLM最高得分仅36.6%；增强型智能体系统达68.8%；社区参考分为95.5%。当前所有模型均远未达到可靠自动化的水平。

Conclusion: 尽管代码生成领域取得了进展，但实现可靠、细粒度的Office文档自动化对当前LLM和智能体系统来说仍是一项重大挑战。

Abstract: The deployment of Large Language Model (LLM) agents for computer automation is accelerating, yet their ability to navigate complex, professional-grade productivity software is largely untested. We argue that Office automation is an ideal environment for benchmarking document-automation capability, as it requires long-horizon planning and reasoning, precise parameter configuration, and multi-application integration. To quantify this capability, we introduce an evaluation based on China's National Computer Rank Examination (NCRE), featuring 200 comprehensive practical-operation tasks across Word, Excel, and PowerPoint. Each task is scored on a 100-point rubric scale using 7,118 machine-gradable criteria, and Score Rate (SR) denotes the mean percentage of rubric points earned across these tasks. We benchmark 7 frontier LLMs and observe stark limitations: single-turn models score a maximum of 36.6%. A stronger agentic system with execution feedback, iterative repair, and broader Office automation access reaches 68.8%, but remains below the 95.5% community-reference score used as a scoring sanity check. Ultimately, our experiments demonstrate that despite recent advancements in code generation, achieving reliable fine-grained Office document automation remains a significant challenge for current code-generating LLM and agent systems.

</details>


### [144] [Bellman-Taylor Score Decoding for Markov Decision Processes with State-Dependent Feasible Action Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10979)
*Yi Chen,Rushuai Yang,Qiang Chen,Dongyan,Huo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Bellman-Taylor分数解码框架，通过将策略学习迁移到欧几里得分数空间并利用动作解码器保证可行性，使得标准深度强化学习算法能够处理状态依赖且隐式定义的可行动作集，并在排队网络控制问题中取得了近优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运筹学中的许多马尔可夫决策过程具有状态依赖且由各种操作约束隐式定义的可行动作，这导致标准深度强化学习算法难以直接应用（因为其动作接口通常假设固定有限动作目录或简单欧几里得空间）。

Method: 受最优动作价值函数泰勒展开的启发，提出Bellman-Taylor分数解码框架：将策略学习迁移到欧几里得分数空间（latent-score MDP），通过动作解码器保证可行性，从而无需对解码器进行微分即可使用标准DRL算法优化。

Result: 提供性能保证，表明最优性间隙可分解为结构逼近误差和算法学习误差；在排队网络控制问题中，策略学习到状态依赖的基于索引的调度规则，小规模实例近优，大规模系统显著优于基准方法。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了具有复杂约束的MDP中DRL算法难以应用的问题，提供了理论保证并在实践中验证了有效性。

Abstract: Many Markov decision processes (MDPs) in operations research have feasible actions that are state dependent and defined implicitly by various operational constraints. These features make it difficult to use standard deep reinforcement learning (DRL) algorithms, whose action interfaces typically assume either a fixed finite action catalog or a simple Euclidean space. Motivated by a Taylor expansion of the optimal action-value function, we propose Bellman--Taylor score decoding, a framework that moves policy learning to a Euclidean score space while enforcing feasibility through an action decoder. The induced latent-score MDP then can be optimized by standard DRL algorithms without differentiating through the decoder. We provide a performance guarantee showing that the optimality gap of this approach decomposes into a structural approximation error and an algorithmic learning error. Lastly, we apply this framework to a queueing network control problem, where the policy essentially learns a state-dependent index-based dispatching rule. Numerical experiments show near-optimal performance in small instances and considerable improvements over benchmarks in larger systems.

</details>


### [145] [Null-Space Constrained Low-Rank Adaptation for Response-Specified Large Language Model Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10989)
*Bocheng Ju,Jianhua Wang,Chengliang Liu,Xiaolin Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NSRU框架，通过零空间约束的低秩投影方法，在LLM遗忘学习中同时实现安全目标学习、不良响应抑制和知识保留，在TOFU和WMDP上表现优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM遗忘方法要么仅抑制不良回答，要么在指定替代行为时缺乏对更新局部性的有效约束，导致遗忘过程中良性能力受损。

Method: NSRU框架包含三部分：1)构建结构化安全目标响应以指定期望行为；2)从良性隐藏表示中估计每模块保留子空间；3)使用正交投影低秩参数化，将LoRA更新约束在保留子空间的零空间内。

Result: 在TOFU上有效抑制遗忘集知识提取，同时提升保留QA性能、模型效用和安全目标对齐；在WMDP上将危险领域准确率保持在随机猜测水平，同时保持MMLU效用。

Conclusion: 零空间约束的投影更新能在减少保留侧扰动的同时保持编辑方向的有效性，安全目标监督、不良响应抑制、保留损失和零空间投影更新各组成部分具有互补作用。

Abstract: Large language model unlearning aims to suppress designated undesirable knowledge while preserving benign capabilities. Many unlearning objectives focus on suppressing undesired answers, while recent target-guided variants specify replacement behavior but still leave update locality largely unconstrained. This paper introduces \emph{Null-Space Constrained Response-Specified Unlearning} (NSRU), a projection-constrained low-rank framework for controlled LLM unlearning. NSRU uses an explicitly structured safe target response to specify the desired behavior for each forget query, while suppressing the original undesired content. To localize adaptation, NSRU estimates per-module retain subspaces from benign hidden representations and uses an orthogonal-projected low-rank parameterization to confine LoRA updates to the null space of the retain subspace. The resulting objective jointly optimizes safe-target learning, undesired-response suppression, and retention preservation under this constrained parameterization. We provide a local first-order analysis showing that the projected update reduces retain-side perturbations while preserving editable directions for shaping forget-query behavior. Experiments on TOFU show that NSRU effectively suppresses extractable forget-set knowledge while improving retain QA performance, model utility, and safe-target alignment over representative baselines. On WMDP, NSRU keeps hazardous-domain accuracy near the random-choice region while preserving broad and domain-adjacent MMLU utility. Ablation studies support the complementary roles of safe-target supervision, undesired-response suppression, retention loss, and null-space projected updates, while sensitivity and robustness analyses indicate stable behavior across the tested hyperparameter and prompt variations.

</details>


### [146] [Structure from Reasoning, Numbers from Search: On-Premise Open LLMs as Structural Priors for Coupled MIMO Controller Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11015)
*Jiaxuan Chen,Haonan Li,Yang Shu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了开源大语言模型（LLM）在强耦合MIMO工业过程控制器调参中的应用。在简单回路中LLM无优势，但在强耦合四水箱系统中，通过结构先验引导，LLM能以更少样本和可解释性达到与全局优化器相当的优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的分散式自整定方法忽略回路间耦合，而局部数值优化易陷入非凸代价函数的局部最优。作者希望探索无需数学模型且数据保留在本地的开源LLM是否能帮助解决这一问题。

Method: 使用开源LLM作为结构先验，结合经典优化器进行迭代改进。在强耦合四水箱系统中，LLM通过推理提出非对称控制器结构，再由经典优化器精调达到全局最优。

Result: 单回路CSTR中LLM调优（0.162）不如继电器反馈（0.106）；强耦合四水箱中，中继调优（J~28.6）和纯LLM调优（29.7）均不如开环（22.7），而LLM结构先验+优化器达到J~12.0（10/10成功率，vs 0/10），且样本效率是全局优化器的6倍。

Conclusion: 开源LLM在控制器调参中的价值不在于作为优化器，而在于作为一种样本高效且可解释的结构先验，尤其在强耦合高维系统中优势显著。在良性系统中则无额外优势。

Abstract: Tuning controllers for strongly coupled multi-input multi-output (MIMO) industrial processes is hard: decentralized classical auto-tuning ignores loop interaction, and local numerical optimization from natural initializations stalls in the resulting non-convex cost landscape. We ask whether on-premise open-source large language models (LLMs), which keep data on-site and need no plant model, can help. On a single-loop CSTR, classical relay-feedback tuning (IAE 0.106, near the 0.102 optimum) beats an LLM tuner (0.162): for simple loops the LLM adds nothing. The picture inverts on a strongly coupled quadruple-tank with conflicting set-points, scored by a penalized cost J = IAE + lambda*TV(u) that rewards tracking without chattering actuators. There, naive relay tuning (J ~ 28.6) and naive LLM tuning (29.7) are no better than open loop (22.7), and a local optimizer from balanced starts fails in 10/10 runs. A scaffolded open LLM instead reasons about the coupling, proposes the counter-intuitive asymmetric structure, and reaches J ~ 16.9 +/- 0.2 from any start; refining it with a classical optimizer attains the smooth global optimum (J ~ 12.0, 10/10 vs. 0/10), which even applies a non-obvious negative integral correction decentralized tuning cannot. A global optimizer (differential evolution) also reaches this optimum, so the LLM is not the only route; its advantage is sample efficiency and interpretability: a usable controller in 18 evaluations (where the global optimizer is worse than open loop) plus a stated rationale. This edge grows with dimension, reaching ~6x fewer evaluations on a 3x3 plant. The behaviour generalizes across four open models, and on a benign plant the LLM offers no advantage, sharpening the boundary. We contribute a reproducible benchmark delimiting when open LLMs help in control tuning: not as optimizers, but as a sample-efficient, interpretable structural prior.

</details>


### [147] [Superficial Beliefs in LLM Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11016)
*Gabriel Freedman,Francesca Toni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型在二元选择任务中是否仅模仿推理，还是具有系统的决策结构。通过对比模型自述的重要属性与行为模型推断的实际驱动属性，发现LLM的决策具有系统性但言语解释不完整，提出"表面信念"概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM在选项选择时的决策是否具有内在一致的结构，还是仅仅表面模仿合理的推理过程，从而理解LLM决策机制的深度。

Method: 设计合成二元决策场景（属性分级定义选项），通过行为模型拟合历史决策来推断实际驱动属性，并与模型自述的重要性报告及评分法官进行对比。

Result: 行为模型能很好预测保留数据，说明决策具有系统性；但模型自述与实际驱动属性仅部分匹配；该模式在多种扰动下保持稳定。

Conclusion: LLM表现出"表面信念"：决策受概率性局部属性优先级引导，但语言层面仅能有限触及真实驱动因素。

Abstract: We ask whether large language models (LLMs) merely imitate rationales when choosing between two options, or whether their choices reflect a systematic underlying decision structure. Using synthetic binary decision settings in which models choose between profiles defined by graded attributes, we compare the attribute a model says mattered most with the attribute that best explains its choice under a behavioural model fit to prior decisions. The behavioural model predicts held-out choices well, showing that model behaviour is systematically related to the visible attributes rather than being random. However, direct self-reports and a separate score-based judge recover the behaviourally inferred driver only partially. The resulting picture is neither one of arbitrary behaviour nor one of fully articulated belief - outputs are structured enough to support prediction, but explicit reasons track the recovered driver only imperfectly. This qualitative pattern persists across prompt-order and sampling perturbations, alternative behavioural models, targeted occlusion analyses, and structurally varied decision settings. We interpret this as evidence for ``superficial belief'' in LLM decision-making: models behave as if guided by probabilistic local priorities over attributes, while having only limited verbal access to the attributes that drive their decisions.

</details>


### [148] [Workflow-GYM: Towards Long-Horizon Evaluation of Computer-use Agentic tasks in Real-World Professional Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11042)
*Liya Zhu,Jingzhe Ding,Jian Zhang,Jianbo Xue,Shihao Liang,Ge Zhang,Xiang Gao,Qingshui Gu,Mailun Gao,Huimin Che,Yan Zhao,Peiheng Zhou,Haojun Wang,Chaobo Xian,Lili Le,Chi Wu,Yiwei Liu,Shengda Long,Jiale Yang,Fangzhi Xu,Sijin Wu,Haodong Duan,Yi Zhu,Chao He,Zhaojian Li,Minchao Wang,Huan Zhou,Jiani Hou,Chuqian Yu,Weiran Shi,Hongwan Gao,Jiamin Chen,Guanhong Chen,Tingqin Luo,Kaiyuan Zhang,Zhixin Yao,Qing Hua,Yuhao Jiang,Jin Chen,Pu Chen,Zhenyu Hu,Xingyu Li,Zhengxuan Jiang,Meng Cao,Tianfeng Long,Haozhe Wang,Mingzhang Wang,Yichen Zhang,Yiming Dai,Chenchen Zhang,Jiaying Wang,Zhiyong Wu,Shen Yan,Yujia Qin,Wenhao Huang,Zaiyuan Wang,Xiaolong Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了 Workflow-GYM 基准，用于评估 AI 代理在专业领域软件中执行长周期、高价值 GUI 工作流程的能力。最强模型成功率仅略高于 30%，揭示了当前代理在保持长程工作流一致性方面的严重不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GUI 基准主要关注通用软件、简单应用和短周期任务，缺乏对专业领域软件中长周期、高价值工作流的评估，不清楚现代 AI 代理能否自主操作专业软件并完成有经济价值的端到端任务。

Method: 构建 Workflow-GYM 基准，聚焦专业领域和专门软件环境的长周期 GUI 任务，并在多个最新模型上进行大量实验。

Result: 最强模型成功率仅略高于 30%，表明专业长周期 GUI 工作流对当前代理极具挑战性。

Conclusion: 当前代理难以维持长周期工作流一致性，频繁出现工作流阶段遗漏、错误传播、目标漂移以及对专业软件环境理解不足等问题，为下一代 GUI 代理研究指明了关键方向。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed the rapid evolution of AI agents toward handling increasingly complex, real-world tasks. However, existing benchmarks rarely evaluate whether agents can operate graphical user interfaces to complete long-horizon, high-value professional workflows across diverse domains. Current GUI benchmarks still predominantly focus on general-purpose software, relatively simple applications, and short-horizon tasks, leaving it largely unknown whether modern agents can follow user instructions to autonomously operate domain-specific professional software and accomplish economically valuable work in an end-to-end manner. To bridge this gap, we introduce Workflow-GYM, a benchmark for long-horizon GUI tasks centered on professional domains and specialized software environments. Through extensive experiments on state-of-the-art models, we find that even the strongest models achieve only slightly above 30% success rates, highlighting that professional long-horizon GUI workflows remain highly challenging for current GUI agents. Further analysis reveals that current agents struggle to maintain long-horizon workflow consistency, frequently exhibiting workflow stage omission, error propagation, objective drift, and insufficient understanding of professional software environments. Our findings provide important insights into the limitations of current agent systems and suggest key directions for the next generation of GUI-agent research.

</details>


### [149] [What Fits (Into Few Tokens) Doesn't Overfit: Compression and Generalization in ML Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11045)
*Martin Andres Bertran,Aaron Roth,Zhiwei Steven Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过LLM驱动的研究智能体实验，发现输出压缩（极短提示）和输入压缩（1位反馈）几乎不影响高性能模型的搜索与复现，支持“成功策略高度可压缩”的假说，即低复杂度策略是基准驱动ML未过拟合的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究为何基准驱动的机器学习实践中很少出现过拟合，检验“成功策略高度可压缩”这一假说是否成立。

Method: 设计两种信息瓶颈实验：输出压缩（探索者用验证集搜索，复现者仅用极短提示+训练数据复现）和输入压缩（探索者仅接收1位反馈），在8个数据集上测试。

Result: 两种信息瓶颈对性能影响很小；短提示和可压缩反馈足以复现和发现高性能模型；故意诱导过拟合时，短提示无法复现结果。

Conclusion: 成功策略位于低复杂度区域，支持描述长度解释：基准驱动ML未出现过拟合是由于成功策略本身可被高度压缩。

Abstract: Reusing a held-out benchmark adaptively should, in principle, invite overfitting. Yet benchmark-driven machine learning (ML) has produced surprisingly little overfitting in practice. An attractive hypothesis is that successful ML strategies are highly compressible. We study this in the setting of LLM-driven research agents, where the hypothesis becomes directly testable via two complementary information bottlenecks. In \emph{output compression}, an exploration agent adaptively searches for high-performance models using a validation set, and we test whether a fresh ``reproducer agent'' can reproduce its performance given only an extremely short prompt and the training data. In \emph{input compression}, the explorer receives only one-bit feedback indicating whether each submitted model improves on the running best. Across 8 datasets spanning tabular classification, vision, language modeling, diffusion modeling, and reward modeling, we find that these bottlenecks have little effect on performance: short prompts and compressible feedback are sufficient to reproduce and find high-performance models. The hypothesis is falsifiable: when we deliberately induce validation-set overfitting, the results fail to reproduce with short prompts. Taken together, our results support a description-length explanation for the lack of overfitting in benchmark-driven ML: successful strategies occupy a low-complexity region of strategy space.

</details>


### [150] [CIAware-Bench: Benchmarking Control Intervention Awareness Across Frontier LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11063)
*Joachim Schaeffer,Thomas Jiralerspong,Alexander Panfilov,Guillaume Lajoie,Jonas Geiping,Yoshua Bengio,Roland S. Zimmermann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 CIAware-Bench，用于评估模型是否能够察觉自己被控制协议干预（即区分自身轨迹与被修改后的轨迹）。在四个任务域与多种协议下测试了11个前沿模型，发现模型对干预的感知能力整体较低到中等，且受任务域、模型配对等因素影响较大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AI控制协议中，可信模型会监控并修改不可信模型的潜在不安全步骤，但这种干预可能被不可信模型察觉，从而使其推断监控机制并规避控制。因此，衡量模型对控制干预的感知能力至关重要。

Method: 构建CIAware-Bench基准，包含四个任务域（essay writing、BigCodeBench、Bash Arena、SHADE-Arena），并变化轨迹水印、侧任务存在及控制协议设置。通过测试模型能否区分自身轨迹与被干预轨迹（二元分类任务）来评估控制干预感知能力。

Result: 在默认设置下，11个前沿模型的CI感知准确率最高为0.87（随机基准为0.5），呈现低到中等水平；不同任务域和模型配对间差异显著；跨模型家族的检测更容易，表明模型利用了提供商特定的风格或后训练差异。

Conclusion: CI感知不是固定的模型级属性，应针对每一次新模型发布和部署场景进行测量。CIAware-Bench可用于追踪CI感知能力并指导设计更难被检测的控制协议。

Abstract: AI control protocols oversee untrusted models by monitoring their actions and modifying potentially unsafe steps, often using a trusted model. This partially tampers with the untrusted model's trajectory. If the trusted model detects such an intervention, it may infer properties of the monitor and adapt to evade control. We introduce \textbf{CIAware-Bench}, a benchmark for measuring \textbf{c}ontrol \textbf{i}ntervention (CI) awareness across frontier models. CIAware-Bench tests whether models can distinguish their own trajectories from those modified by a control intervention. The benchmark is comprised of a suite of four task domains (essay writing, BigCodeBench, Bash Arena, and SHADE-Arena), while varying trajectory watermarking, side-task presence, and the control protocol. Evaluating eleven frontier models, we find low to moderate CI awareness under default settings (up to 0.87; random chance balanced binary classification accuracy is 0.5) with substantial variation across task domains and model pairs. Detection is generally easier across model families, suggesting that models exploit provider-specific differences in style or post-training. Overall, CI awareness is not a fixed model-level property, and should be measured for each new model release and deployment scenario. We release CIAware-Bench to track CI awareness and inform control protocols whose interventions are harder to detect.

</details>


### [151] [A History-Aware Visually Grounded Critic for Computer Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11078)
*Jaewoo Lee,Zaid Khan,Archiki Prasad,Justin Chih-Yao Chen,Supriyo Chakraborty,Kartik Balasubramaniam,Sambit Sahu,Elias Stengel-Eskin,Hyunji Lee,Mohit Bansal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HiViG是一个历史感知、视觉基元的测试时干预框架，通过多模态批评模型抽象历史交互、提供宏观动作历史和视觉基元批评，在Web、移动和桌面GUI基准测试中显著提升了计算机使用智能体的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机使用智能体的批评模型存在两大局限：(1)短视决策循环，容易遗忘早期动作；(2)缺乏视觉基元能力，无法检测错误操作（如点击错误UI元素）。

Method: 提出HiViG框架，包含：(1)基于真实GUI轨迹训练的多模态批评模型，用于抽象历史交互为紧凑记录并视觉基元评估动作；(2)宏观动作历史提供策略已完成成就的总结；(3)视觉基元批评通过验证原始执行坐标与当前截图来拦截错误。

Result: 在Web、移动和桌面基准测试中，HiViG优于现有标量和口头批评方法，在最强基线上提升Qwen3-VL-32B成功率5.8%、Gemini-3-Flash成功率9.0%，并展示出强跨平台泛化能力。

Conclusion: 宏观动作历史缓解了短视规划，视觉基元批评减少了执行错误，两者对长时域GUI任务中的测试时扩展都至关重要。

Abstract: Various test-time interventions for Computer Use Agents (CUAs), including critic models, have been developed to improve performance through pre-execution action evaluation in complex Graphical User Interface (GUI) environments. However, existing critics suffer from two key limitations: they (1) focus primarily on short-sighted decision loops (e.g., forgetting earlier actions) and (2) lack the visual grounding needed to detect flawed actions (e.g., clicking wrong UI elements). To address these, we introduce HiViG, a History-aware Visually Grounded test-time framework, built around a multimodal critic trained on real GUI trajectories to abstract past interactions into a compact record and to evaluate actions with visual grounding. At test time, HiViG integrates the critic into the policy decision loop to provide macro-action history, which summarizes the policy's completed achievements, and visually grounded critique, which verifies raw execution coordinates against the current screenshot to intercept errors before execution. Across web, mobile, and desktop benchmarks, HiViG consistently outperforms existing scalar and verbal critics, improving average success rates over the strongest baseline by 5.8% for Qwen3-VL-32B and 9.0% for Gemini-3-Flash, and demonstrates strong cross-platform generalization. Ablations show that macro-action history mitigates short-sighted planning and visually grounded critique reduces execution errors, with both components being critical for test-time scaling in long-horizon GUI tasks.

</details>


### [152] [Monte Carlo Pass Search: Using Trajectory Generation for 3D Counterfactual Pass Evaluation in Football](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11120)
*Andrew Kang,Priya Narasimhan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出蒙特卡洛传球搜索（MCPS），将足球传球评估转化为类似蒙特卡洛树搜索的评估问题，结合价值模型、世界模型和反事实策略，在德甲3D轨迹数据集上实现传球价值的分布感知归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有足球传球评估方法缺乏对反事实执行变体的系统采样，难以衡量传球决策的分布特征而非单一点估计。

Method: 构建MCPS框架：先推断传球参数，采样执行变体和选项变体，用条件化的世界模型推演至下一次球交互，再用学习的价值模型评分，得到价值分布并计算两种执行剩余分数（均值和百分位数）。

Result: 在德甲高保真3D轨迹数据集上，采用离散令牌自回归轨迹生成器（SMART）的世界模型在最佳前20的预测准确率上优于基线。

Conclusion: MCPS实现了分布感知的传球价值归因，提供了比传统方法更丰富的统计视角，且代码和模型已开源。

Abstract: We recast pass evaluation in football (soccer) as a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS)-like evaluation problem whose components mostly exist in the literature under different names: a value model (possession value), a world model (multi-agent trajectories with ball interactions), and a policy over counterfactual actions (sampling pass variants with noise). Building on the first public high-fidelity tracking dataset with 3D ball trajectories from the Bundesliga, we introduce Monte Carlo Pass Search (MCPS), which infers kick parameters for each observed pass, samples execution variants and option variants, rolls each candidate forward with a ball-conditioned world model until the next ball interaction, and scores outcomes with a learned value model to obtain a distribution over gained value. This distribution enables distribution-aware attribution with two complementary execution-surplus scores used for analysis and ranking: mean-based and percentile-based scores. To make the world model sample-efficient under limited public data, we adapt a discrete-token, autoregressive trajectory generator from autonomous driving (SMART) and show it yields strong best-of-20 forecasting accuracy compared to baselines, while supporting fully hypothetical rollouts for downstream evaluation. We have released model checkpoints and code.

</details>


### [153] [ABC-Bench: An Agentic Bio-Capabilities Benchmark for Biosecurity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11150)
*Andrew Bo Liu,Samira Nedungadi,Bryce Cai,Alex Kleinman,Harmon Bhasin,Seth Donoughe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ABC-Bench基准测试，用于评估LLM在生物安全相关任务中的能力。测试发现，所有LLM在液体处理机器人编程、DNA片段设计和DNA合成筛选规避三项任务上均优于人类专家中位数，且o4-mini-high生成的脚本在实际湿实验中成功组装了目标DNA序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在生物学领域的快速进步，其既能推动科学发现，也带来了生物安全风险。现有评估缺乏针对生物安全的代理能力指标，因此需要建立系统化的基准来量化这些风险。

Method: 设计ABC-Bench基准测试套件，包含三项双用途任务：液体处理机器人编程（操作OpenTrons）、DNA片段设计（体外组装）、DNA合成筛选规避。同时进行湿实验验证LLM生成脚本的实际可执行性。

Result: 所有LLM在三项任务上均超越人类专家中位数，但在需要新颖生物信息学推理的任务上表现较弱。o4-mini-high的脚本在湿实验中成功组装出预期序列的DNA。

Conclusion: LLM已具备显著的生物安全相关代理能力，尤其在遵循已发表知识和标准协议的任务上表现突出，但在需要创新性推理的任务上仍有局限。这为生物安全政策制定和风险缓解提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are rapidly acquiring capabilities relevant to biological research, from literature synthesis to interpretation of experimental data. Increasingly, LLM agents can also perform in silico biology tasks that previously required experienced human biologists. These emerging AI capabilities offer new opportunities for scientific discovery and biomedical advances, but they also shift the landscape of biosecurity risks. To address this, we introduce the Agentic Bio-Capabilities Benchmark (ABC-Bench), a suite of tasks to measure agentic biosecurity-relevant capabilities. ABC-Bench evaluates LLM agents on both benign and dual-use biology tasks: writing code to operate liquid handling robots, designing DNA fragments for in vitro assembly, and evading DNA synthesis screening. These tasks require a combination of biology and software expertise. All tested LLM agents outperformed the median expert human baseliner on all three tasks. Agents performed highly on tasks drawing on published knowledge and well-documented protocols, and more weakly on a task requiring novel bioinformatics reasoning. In three wet-lab validation experiments, we found that OpenAI's o4-mini-high produced scripts that, when run on an OpenTrons liquid handling robot, successfully assembled DNA with expected sequences.

</details>


### [154] [ReasonAlloc: Hierarchical Decoding-Time KV Cache Budget Allocation for Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11164)
*Wenhao Liu,Hao Shi,Yunhe Li,Weizhi Fei,Xiangyuan Wang,Mengzhe Ruan,Hanxu Hou,Peisong Wang,Linqi Song,Shuang Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ReasonAlloc，一种无需训练的分层预算分配框架，通过离线层级别预分配（捕捉"推理波"模式）和在线头级别重分配，解决长思维链推理中KV缓存压缩问题。在数学推理基准上优于均匀预算方法，尤其在低预算场景下表现显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长思维链推理中KV缓存快速增长导致推理瓶颈，现有解码时压缩方法采用均匀预算分配，未考虑层与头的差异化需求；而现有非均匀预算方法仅针对静态预填充阶段设计，无法捕捉自回归解码的逐步上下文需求。

Method: 提出ReasonAlloc，双层预算分配框架：(1) 离线层级别预分配：捕捉架构驱动的"推理波"需求模式；(2) 在线头级别重分配：基于实时效用将资源重新分配给信息丰富的注意力头。

Result: 在MATH-500、AIME 2024上使用DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B等模型评估，ReasonAlloc优于R-KV、SnapKV、Pyramid-RKV等基线，低预算（128-512 tokens）下增益最大，且可与现有token驱逐策略即插即用。

Conclusion: ReasonAlloc通过分层非均匀预算分配有效缓解长思维链推理的KV缓存瓶颈，开销可忽略，为解码时KV压缩提供了一个高效且通用的框架。

Abstract: Long chain-of-thought (CoT) trajectories in large language model (LLM) reasoning cause severe inference bottlenecks due to rapid key-value (KV) cache growth. Current decoding-time compression methods mitigate this issue via token eviction, but typically assume a uniform budget distribution across all layers and heads. In contrast, existing non-uniform budget allocation methods are predominantly designed for the static prompt prefill phase, and they do not capture the stepwise context demands of autoregressive reasoning. To bridge this gap, we propose ReasonAlloc, a training-free framework that recasts decoding-time KV compression as a hierarchical budget allocation problem. ReasonAlloc operates at two complementary levels: an offline layer-wise preallocation strategy captures an architecture-driven demand pattern which we call ``\textit{Reasoning Wave}'', while an online head-wise strategy reallocates resources during decoding to information-rich heads based on real-time utility. Evaluations on mathematical reasoning benchmarks (MATH-500, AIME~2024) using DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B, DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B, and AceReason-14B show that ReasonAlloc outperforms uniform-budget R-KV, SnapKV, and Pyramid-RKV (a baseline enforcing a static, monotonically decreasing layer budget), with the largest gains at small budgets (128-512 tokens). ReasonAlloc is plug-and-play with existing token-eviction policies and introduces negligible inference-time overhead.

</details>


### [155] [The Role of Feedback Alignment in Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11173)
*Semih Kara,Oğuzhan Ersoy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究自蒸馏（self-distillation）中上下文设计的影响，发现与求解器推理轨迹步骤对齐的批评（step-aligned critique）作为上下文效果最佳，比GRPO高出16.11分，比参考解条件高出5.27分。原因是步骤对齐的反馈只针对推理失败的token，保留正确行为，而参考解条件会迫使模型在所有token上改变行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自蒸馏方法通过让模型在无上下文时模仿有上下文的输出分布来提升性能，但给自教师（self-teacher）提供什么样的上下文（context）这一设计空间尚未被充分探索。

Method: 使用冻结的评判器（frozen critic）提供反馈，在数学求解任务上比较三种上下文条件：(i) 二元奖励（GRPO），(ii) 参考解，(iii) 与求解器推理轨迹步骤对齐的批评。

Result: 步骤对齐批评效果最佳，Avg@12指标上比GRPO高16.11分，比参考解条件高5.27分。每token优势分析表明步骤对齐反馈仅针对推理失败的token，而参考解条件迫使模型在每个token上改变行为。

Conclusion: 反馈与求解器推理的结构对齐（structural alignment）是自蒸馏效果的关键驱动因素。

Abstract: Conditioning a language model on additional context, such as feedback on a previous attempt, typically improves its response. Self-distillation trains the model to retain this improvement when the context is not present. The method works by matching the model's output distribution under two settings: a student that sees only the question, and a self-teacher that also sees the context. What the model learns therefore depends on what context the self-teacher receives, yet the design of this context remains largely unexplored.
  We study context design for self-distillation by training a solver on feedback from a frozen critic. We compare three conditions: (i) a binary reward (GRPO), (ii) the reference solution, and (iii) a step-by-step critique aligned to the solver's reasoning trace.
  Step-aligned critique yields the largest gains, outperforming GRPO by 16.11 points and reference-solution-conditioned self-distillation by 5.27 points (Avg@12). Per-token advantage analysis reveals why: step-aligned feedback targets only the tokens where reasoning fails, leaving correct behavior intact. Conditioning on the reference solution, by contrast, pressures the model to change its behavior at every token (even correct steps) because an alternative derivation inevitably differs in phrasing and approach. This suggests that structural alignment between feedback and the solver's reasoning is a key driver of self-distillation effectiveness.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [Uncertainty-Aware Motion Planning for Autonomous Driving in Mixed Traffic Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09958)
*Ming Cheng,Hao Chen,Ziyi Yang,Ziluowen Luo,Senzhang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出UAMP方法，通过量化人类驾驶员意图的不确定性并引入不确定性校准的价值学习，提升自动驾驶在混合交通场景中的安全性和舒适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法将预测的人类意图视为确定状态，忽略了意图预测固有的不确定性（行为多样性、感知噪声、部分可观测性），导致不安全决策。

Method: 1) 邻近感知不确定性估计器（量化交互条件下的意图不确定性）；2) 不确定性引导的联合意图分布构建；3) 不确定性校准的价值学习（UCVL，修正因直接引入不确定性意图导致的价值函数学习偏差）。

Result: 在多种混合交通场景的实验中，UAMP相比现有方法显著提升了安全性和驾驶舒适性，同时保持了交通效率。

Conclusion: 将意图不确定性显式建模并融入自动驾驶决策框架是提升安全性的有效途径，UAMP为此提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In mixed-traffic environments where autonomous and human-driven vehicles may co-exist, motion planning for autonomous vehicles requires anticipating the future behaviors of surrounding human drivers. Existing reinforcement learning-based methods generally directly incorporate the predicted human intents into the observation to enable a proactive planning. However, human intent is inherently uncertain due to the behavioral diversity, perception noise, and partial observability. Treating predicted intends as deterministic states can result in unsafe decisions for autonomous vehicles. To address this problem, we propose Uncertainty-Aware Motion Planning (UAMP), which incorporates uncertainty in human intent prediction for AV decision-making. Specifically, UAMP first introduces a proximity-aware uncertainty estimator to quantify the interaction-conditioned intent uncertainty and constructs an uncertainty-guided joint intent distribution over surrounding human-driven vehicles. Within this uncertainty set, UAMP further introduces Uncertainty-Calibrated Value Learning (UCVL) to correct value function learning biases arising from directly incorporating uncertain human intent predictions into the observation. Extensive experiments in various mixed-traffic scenarios show that UAMP significantly improves safety and driving comfort, while maintaining traffic efficiency compared with existing approaches. The code is released at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/UAMP-5638.

</details>


### [157] [GHOST: Hierarchical Sub-Goal Policies for Generalizing Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10025)
*Sriram Krishna,Ben Eisner,Haotian Zhan,Ying Yuan,Haoyu Zhen,Chuang Gan,Shubham Tulsiani,David Held*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GHOST是一个分层框架，通过将控制分解为高层策略（从多视角RGB-D观测预测3D末端执行器位姿的下一子目标）和低层目标条件控制器，实现了视觉运动操作策略的泛化。该方法使用3D目标投影到图像平面形成末端执行器热图作为空间接口，并在多种操作任务中持续优于扁平扩散策略。此外，该分层接口便于融入人类演示（无需动作重定向），高层策略可在人类视频上训练以指定技能应用方式，低层策略仅用机器人数据训练，从而通过少量人类演示实现对新物体和任务变体的适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉运动操作策略（如扁平扩散策略）在训练分布外泛化能力有限，且难以有效利用人类演示数据（因动作重定向存在噪声）。作者希望设计一种能超越训练分布泛化、且能自然融合人类演示的框架。

Method: 提出GHOST分层框架：1）高层策略：从多视角RGB-D观测预测下一子目标（3D末端执行器位姿分布）；2）低层策略：执行具体实施例动作的目标条件控制器。关键设计是将3D子目标投影到图像平面形成末端执行器热图作为空间接口。高层策略可基于人类视频训练（子目标与实施例无关），低层策略仅用机器人数据训练。

Result: 在多种操作任务上，GHOST的分层因子化方法一致优于扁平扩散策略的性能和鲁棒性。同时，该方法能有效利用人类演示数据（无需动作重定向），通过少量人类演示即可适应新物体和任务变体。

Conclusion: 分层因子化控制策略能有效提升视觉运动操作的泛化能力和鲁棒性，且通过将高层策略与实施例无关的子目标预测分离，可自然融合不同来源的演示数据（人类视频+机器人数据），显著降低对新任务和新物体的适应成本。

Abstract: We present GHOST, a framework for learning visuomotor manipulation policies that generalize beyond the training distribution. GHOST factorizes control into (i) a high-level policy that predicts the next sub-goal as a distribution over 3D end-effector poses from multi-view RGB-D observations, and (ii) a low-level goal-conditioned controller that executes embodiment-specific actions. To condition image-based policies on 3D goals, we introduce a simple spatial interface that projects predicted goals into the image plane and represents them as end-effector heatmaps. Across a suite of manipulation tasks, this hierarchical factorization consistently improves performance and robustness compared to a flat Diffusion Policy.
  Further, we show that this hierarchical interface also makes it easy to incorporate human demonstrations without relying on (noisy) action retargeting. As sub-goals are largely embodiment-agnostic, we train the high-level policy on human video to specify how learned skills should be applied and composed, while keeping the low-level policy trained purely on robot data. This hierarchy enables adaptation to novel objects and task variations using a small number of human demonstrations.

</details>


### [158] [Robotic Nonprehensile Object Transportation with a Hanging Tray](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10039)
*Adam Heins,Angela P. Schoellig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出使用绳索悬挂托盘（三维摆）解决机器人侍者问题，相比刚性抓取托盘，仅需3自由度移动基座即可实现类似摆动的减震效果，显著减少物体滑动和液体泼溅，并集成视觉伺服完成交互式服务演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统刚性托盘需要6自由度机械臂模拟摆运动以减少剪切力，成本高且复杂。作者希望用更简单的3自由度移动基座+真实悬挂托盘获得同样的减震优势。

Method: 将托盘通过绳索悬挂于机器人末端，形成三维摆系统；仅控制移动基座（3DOF）运动；通过视觉识别举手用户并用视觉伺服导航靠近。

Result: 仿真和实物实验表明，悬挂托盘相比刚性抓取托盘在减少物体滑动和液体泼溅方面显著更优。

Conclusion: 悬挂托盘是机器人侍者问题的有效方案，能以更低自由度硬件实现类似摆动的减震效果，适合实际交互服务场景。

Abstract: We consider the nonprehensile object transportation task known as the waiter's problem, in which a robot must move an object balanced on a tray from one location to another. In contrast to prior works on the robotic waiter's problem, which make the robot tilt a tray rigidly held by its end effector (EE), we use a tray suspended from the EE by ropes, such that it behaves like a three-dimensional pendulum. Some prior works have actuated the robot so that the EE simulates the behavior of a pendulum, because pendular motion reduces the shear forces acting on the transported objects, minimizing the sliding of rigid objects and sloshing in containers of liquid. In contrast, our use of a real hanging tray allows us to obtain the benefits of pendular motion while only actuating a 3 degree-of-freedom (DOF) mobile base, rather than requiring a full 6-DOF manipulator arm. Our experiments in simulation and on real hardware show that the hanging tray substantially reduces both sliding and sloshing compared to a static, rigidly-grasped tray. Furthermore, we integrate the hanging tray into an interactive robot waiter demonstration, which uses computer vision to identify people with a raised hand and visual servoing to steer toward them and allow them to access the tray.

</details>


### [159] [Efficient-WAM: A 1B-Parameter World-Action Model with Low-Cost Future Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10040)
*Jiajun Li,Tiecheng Guo,Yifan Ye,Rongyu Zhang,Xiaowei Chi,Qianpu Sun,Ying Li,Yunfan Lou,Yan Huang,Zhihe Lu,Meng Guo,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Efficient-WAM通过紧凑视频专家、令牌稀疏潜变量和非对称视频-动作去噪，在保持控制性能的同时将推理延迟降低30倍（约100ms/块），解决了世界动作模型实时部署的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型依赖逼真的未来预测，推理延迟高，难以实时部署于机器人。因此需要设计更高效的WAM，在保持未来预测控制收益的同时降低推理成本。

Method: 1）从WAN-2.2-5B迁移紧凑视频专家；2）令牌稀疏的视频潜变量表示；3）非对称视频-动作去噪（视频分配更少采样步数）；4）未来视频预测作为动作生成的紧凑引导信号而非追求视觉保真度。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0和真实操作任务中，尽管未来预测较粗糙，仍保持强动作性能；1B参数模型将每块延迟降至约100ms，相比现有WAM实现30倍加速。

Conclusion: 通过牺牲未来预测的视觉保真度换取推理效率，证明紧凑的未来预测足以作为有效的动作控制引导信号，为WAM的实时机器人部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: World-Action Models (WAMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for embodied control by coupling future visual prediction with action generation. However, most existing WAMs rely on photorealistic future prediction, which incurs high inference latency and makes real-time robot deployment difficult. This motivates a more efficient WAM design that preserves the control benefits of future visual prediction while reducing its inference cost. We introduce Efficient-WAM, a World-Action Model that reduces the cost of future imagination while preserving its control benefit. Efficient-WAM improves inference efficiency via a compact video expert transferred from WAN-2.2-5B, token-sparse video latents, and asymmetric video-action denoising that allocates fewer sampling steps to video than to actions. Instead of optimizing the future branch for visual fidelity, Efficient-WAM treats future video prediction as a compact guidance signal for action generation. Comprehensive experiments on RoboTwin 2.0 and real-world manipulation tasks show that Efficient-WAM maintains strong action performance despite visibly coarse future predictions. While maintaining competitive control capabilities, our 1B-parameter model can reduce per-chunk latency to around 100 ms during physical deployment, achieving a 30x speedup over existing WAMs.

</details>


### [160] [Flow Control: Steering Vision-Language-Action Models with Simple Real-Time Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10180)
*Jonathan C. Kao,Jason Chan,Andy Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过通用输入（如键盘）实时控制VLA模型动作的流控制方法，无需重新训练即可让用户干预机器人的动作生成，同时保证动作质量和用户意图一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在机器人任务中表现优异，但难以在运行中根据用户实时意图进行调整。现有方法需要重新训练或微调，缺乏灵活的人机交互手段。

Method: 通过流控制技术，将用户的粗粒度输入（如键盘指令）转化为从VLA专家动作分布中采样的高质量动作样本，实现用户意图与专家知识的融合。

Result: (1) 能准确、响应式地控制机器人动作；(2) 对次优用户输入具有鲁棒性；(3) 显著提高了任务成功率和完成速度；(4) 基于流控制轨迹微调可提升自主策略性能。

Conclusion: 流控制是一种简单、即用型的人机交互方法，无需额外训练即可有效提升VLA的任务表现，为机器人实时人机协作提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce flow control of vision-language-action (VLA) models, a simple and effective way to steer VLA actions in real-time through generic inputs, such as a keyboard. This method can be used out-of-the-box and does not require retraining or fine-tuning VLAs. It enables relatively crude user inputs to steer a VLA to align with user intent. The VLA transforms these inputs into action samples drawn from the VLA expert action distribution learned during training, so that the generated actions are high quality (conformity to the action expert distribution) and high fidelity (reflecting the user's intent). We demonstrate that flow control has many desirable properties: (1) flow control accurately and responsively steers robot actions with user inputs, (2) it is robust to suboptimal user inputs, (3) it enables users to steer VLAs to achieve significantly higher success rates and faster task completion, and (4) fine-tuning a VLA on flow control trajectories improves the autonomous policy. Together, these results provide a simple and intuitive way for users to help steer VLA actions, increasing task performance.

</details>


### [161] [Exploration of Foundation Model-Based Robots in Patient and Elderly Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10208)
*Zhiwen Qiu,Wei Liu,Yuexing Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于基础模型的护理机器人现状，指出其在语音交互和社交辅助方面有进展，但多模态感知和物理自主性有限，可靠性问题（如幻觉）依然存在，临床护理效果证据不足。未来应转向护理特定的评估标准、可问责自主性以及与护理工作流的整合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着全球人口老龄化，老年人和患者护理需求快速增长。基础模型被越来越多地集成到机器人中，但护理场景要求可靠、与工作流兼容且有人类监督的系统，当前具身系统能否将技术进展转化为临床影响尚不明确。

Method: 本文是一篇Perspective（观点综述），从三个维度（设计特征、用户体验、护理相关结果证据）综合分析了基于基础模型的护理机器人现状。

Result: 当前系统主要将基础模型用作语音社交辅助机器人中的对话和推理层，多模态接地和物理自主性仍有限。实证评估报告了积极的可用性和参与度，但可靠性故障（如幻觉、对话中断）普遍存在。护理影响的证据仍集中在认知参与等近端结果上，缺乏验证过的临床或护理相关变化证据。

Conclusion: 未来研究应转向护理特定的评估标准、可问责的自主性，以及与护理工作流的整合，以开发更具响应性和责任感的护理技术。

Abstract: Demand for older-adult and patient care is growing rapidly as populations age worldwide. Foundation models are increasingly being integrated into robots and interactive agents, with the promise of more flexible communication and personalized assistance. However, care settings require reliable and workflow-compatible systems with accountable human oversight, and it remains unclear whether current embodied systems can translate technical advances into clinical impact. This Perspective synthesizes foundation model-based care robots across three areas: design features, user experience, and evidence for care-related outcomes. Current systems most commonly use foundation models as conversational and reasoning layers within voice-centered socially assistive embodiments, while multimodal grounding and physical autonomy remain limited. Empirical evaluations report positive usability and engagement benefits, but reliability failures persist across the interaction pipeline such as hallucinations and conversational breakdowns. Evidence for care impact remains concentrated in proximal outcomes such as cognitive engagement and participation, with limited evidence for validated clinical or care-related changes. We argue that future research should transition toward care-specific evaluation standards, accountable autonomy, and integration into care workflows to support more responsive and responsible care technologies.

</details>


### [162] [What Demonstration Curation Metrics Do to Your Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10229)
*Aarav Bedi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 行为克隆策略的演示数据筛选质量不应仅用缺陷检测指标衡量，而应直接评估最终策略性能。实验发现，高AUROC(0.804)的筛选指标反而产生最差策略（13.3%成功率），而低AUROC(0.638)的指标几乎达到oracle水平（90.0% vs 93.3%）。七种指标中有五种将episode长度作为缺陷的混淆代理变量，导致AUROC虚高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究演示数据筛选指标（用于检测缺陷样本）与下游行为克隆策略性能之间的关联是否一致，以及常用的AUROC指标是否存在误导性。

Method: 在LIBERO接触性拾取-放置基准任务上，人为注入结构化缺陷（过早释放夹爪），评估七种不同的筛选指标，对比其缺陷检测AUROC与经过筛选数据训练的策略成功率。

Result: 1) 缺陷检测AUROC与最终策略性能严重脱钩；2) 五种指标利用episode长度作为缺陷的混淆代理变量；3) 控制episode长度后AUROC大幅下降；4) 最佳筛选方法将策略成功率从3.3%（未筛选）提升至接近oracle的90.0%。

Conclusion: 筛选方法应直接评估其产生的策略性能，而非缺陷检测准确率；任何筛选基准必须控制episode长度这一混淆因素。

Abstract: We study whether demonstration-curation metrics that detect defective training episodes also improve the downstream behavior-cloning policy that trains on the curated data. On a contact-rich LIBERO pick-and-place benchmark with a controlled structural defect (early gripper release during the carry phase), we find that the two quantities are sharply decoupled. The metric with the highest defect-detection AUROC (0.804) produces the worst curated policy (13.3% task success), while a metric with a substantially lower AUROC (0.638) produces a policy that nearly matches the oracle trained on ground-truth clean data (90.0% vs. 93.3%). We further show that five of the seven metrics we evaluate exploit episode length as a trivial proxy for the defect label, a confound that inflates reported AUROCs to near-perfect values and disappears once episode length is controlled. Across all conditions, the contaminated baseline succeeds on only 3.3% of rollouts, and the two best curation methods close this to within 3 percentage points of the 93.3% oracle ceiling. Our results argue that curation methods should be evaluated by the policy they produce, not the defects they flag, and that any curation benchmark must control for episode length before reporting detection accuracy. We release the testbed, all metric implementations, and the evaluation pipeline.

</details>


### [163] [YUBI: Yielding Universal Bidigital Interface for Bimanual Dexterous Manipulation at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10244)
*Takehiko Ohkawa,Jumpei Arima,Yuki Noguchi,Masatoshi Tateno,Makoto Sugiura,Takuya Okubo,Kengo Ikeuchi,Yuma Shin,Hiroki Nishizawa,Naoaki Kanazawa,Yuki Wakayama,Daiki Fukunaga,Koshi Makihara,Tomohiro Motoda,Floris Erich,Yukiyasu Domae,Tatsuya Matsushima,Yohishiro Okumatsu,Kei Ota*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出YUBI——一种手指对齐的平行夹爪，通过顺应性手指驱动设计将人类手指运动直接映射到夹爪运动，实现双手灵巧操作的直观、人体工学且可扩展的数据采集。基于YUBI构建了包含8,434小时、120万条轨迹、119个任务的大规模数据集，训练出的单一策略可直接迁移至多种双臂机器人平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UMI手持数据采集系统的手枪式握把在精细灵巧操作任务中存在人体工学差、易疲劳、操作不便等问题，亟需一种更贴合人类手指运动、更直觉化的数据采集接口。

Method: YUBI夹爪采用"顺应性手指驱动"设计，将人类手指运动直接映射到夹爪开合；结合VR头显实现夹爪6DoF跟踪；在119个任务上采集了120万条轨迹、8,434小时的数据。

Result: 相比UMI夹爪，YUBI在复杂双手任务的通用性、灵巧性和操作效率上表现更优；单一策略在UR、Franka、ELEY多种机器人上直接迁移成功，无需额外适配。

Conclusion: YUBI提供了一个可复现的大规模数据采集方案，硬件、软件和数据集全部开源，为推进机器人基础模型研究奠定了数据基础设施。

Abstract: We introduce Yielding Universal Bidigital Interface (YUBI), a finger-aligned gripper designed to enable intuitive, ergonomic, and scalable data collection for bimanual dexterous manipulation. While handheld data collection systems such as Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI) enable affordable data collection, their bulky pistol-grip designs can pose ergonomic and usability challenges for fine-grained, dexterous manipulation tasks. To address this, YUBI presents a distinct design principle: yielding, finger-driven actuation that directly maps human finger movements to gripper jaw motion. Using the YUBI devices, we set up a data collection system with integrated VR-based 6 DoF tracking of the gripper, ensuring high-fidelity trajectory data acquisition. We curate a UMI-based dataset of unprecedented scale: 8,434 hours across 1.20M episodes and 119 tasks. Experiments show that YUBI offers advantages over the UMI gripper in versatility for complex bimanual tasks, dexterity, and operational efficiency. A single policy trained on the YUBI dataset transfers across multiple bimanual robots (UR, Franka, and ELEY) simply by mounting the gripper on each platform, confirming that the collected data are directly executable as policy supervision. We release the gripper hardware, data-collection software, and dataset as one integrated stack, offering the open community a reproducible path to large-scale data acquisition for advancing robotic foundation models.

</details>


### [164] [What Matters in Orchestrating Robot Policies: A Systematic Study of Hierarchical VLA Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10267)
*Jiaheng Hu,Mohit Shridhar,Caden Lu,Dhruv Shah,Hao-Tien Lewis Chiang,Jie Tan,Annie Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了层次化视觉-语言-动作（Hi-VLA）系统的设计原则，通过统一的选项式控制框架对现有系统进行基准测试，提炼出构建高效Hi-VLA系统的实用设计原则，并在仿真和真实机器人上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Hi-VLA系统在规划器与控制器选择、切换机制、观察和记忆表示等方面缺乏统一的设计原则，导致系统设计缺乏指导性依据。

Method: 将代表性Hi-VLA智能体统一到选项式控制框架下，在短周期、长周期和推理密集型任务上对核心设计选择进行系统基准测试，从而提炼设计原则。

Result: 提出的设计原则能显著提升系统性能，在仿真和真实ALOHA机器人实验中，均优于扁平VLA控制或朴素层次化设计。

Conclusion: 本文为构建更强大、更鲁棒、更有原则性的层次化VLA智能体提供了基础性指导。

Abstract: Hierarchical vision-language-action (Hi-VLA) systems have emerged as a promising paradigm for complex robot manipulation, by using high-level VLM planners to decompose tasks into language subgoals executed by low-level VLA controllers. Despite recent empirical progress, there is a lack of unified design principles for these systems: existing Hi-VLA systems differ in how they choose and connect planners, controllers, mechanisms to switch between the two, and how observations and memory are represented in the planner. In this paper, we present a systematic study of Hi-VLA design for robot manipulation. We unify representative Hi-VLA agents under an options-style control framework and benchmark core design choices across short-horizon, long-horizon, and reasoning-intensive tasks. Our analysis distills practical principles for building Hi-VLA systems, showing how model choices and interface mechanisms jointly shape performance. Applying these principles yields a substantially stronger system than either flat VLA control or a naively designed hierarchy, across experiments both in simulation and on a real ALOHA robot. Overall, our results provide a foundation for building more capable, robust, and principled hierarchical VLA agents. More information and video at jiahenghu.github.io/hi-vla.

</details>


### [165] [Locomotion analysis of a quadruped interacting with the lunar granular surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10273)
*Yash J Vyas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在模拟月球颗粒土壤（风化层）环境中，使用强化学习训练四足机器人运动策略时，刚性接触与软接触模型对能耗、步态和训练效果的影响。结果表明，软接触环境会增加训练难度、改变步态特征并提高能量消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将腿足机器人部署到地外环境（如月球）面临复杂地形、能量和热约束等挑战。月球表面由颗粒状风化层构成，对机器人 locomotion 性能影响显著。基于刚性接触假设训练的运动算法在软接触环境下效果不佳，可能导致不稳定和跟踪误差，因此需要研究软接触对强化学习训练的影响。

Method: 1）对月球颗粒土壤与机器人足部接触进行物理建模，并将该模型集成到仿真环境中；2）使用强化学习分别训练刚性接触环境和软接触环境下的运动策略；3）对比两种策略下的步态和 locomotion 性能指标。

Result: 1）软接触模拟风化层表面给强化学习训练带来额外挑战；2）软接触环境下训练出的策略在步态上具有定性差异；3）整体能量消耗增加。

Conclusion: 月球风化层软接触环境对强化学习训练的运动策略有显著影响，需要在仿真中准确建模颗粒土壤接触特性，以提高地外腿足机器人的实际部署性能。

Abstract: Deploying legged robots in extra-terrestrial environments includes many challenges due to complex terrain interactions, energy, and thermal constraints. For effective mechanical design of a lunar exploration quadrupedal robot, careful consideration of motor torques, energy expenditure, and cost of transport is required. The lunar surface is composed of granular regolith, which impacts the locomotion of legged robots and their performance. Locomotion algorithms trained with rigid contact assumptions are also ineffective when applied to environments with soft contacts, such as granular surfaces, which can result in instability and poor tracking. In this report, the physical modelling of the granular lunar surface-robot foot contacts is applied to a simulation environment with locomotion trained using Reinforcement Learning. A comparison is conducted between the policy trained on rigid contact and soft contact environments, analysing the gait and locomotion performance metrics. The analysis demonstrates that soft contacts simulating regolith surfaces pose additional challenges for Reinforcement Learning based training, result in a qualitatively different gait, and increase the overall energy expenditure.

</details>


### [166] [Hierarchical Policies from Verbal and Egocentric Human Signals for Natural Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10276)
*Dongjun Lee,Juheon Choi,Dong Kyu Shin,Sinjae Kang,Kimin Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EDITH是一个利用智能眼镜捕捉人类第一人称视角、目光和语音等非语言信号，结合语言指令来驱动机器人策略的框架。它通过分层策略（高层推断意图并生成子任务，低层执行）实现更自然的人机交互，显著减少用户表达意图所需努力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人策略仅依赖语言指令作为意图传达的唯一接口，忽略了手势、目光等非语言信号，将全部沟通负担压在用户身上，缺乏自然的人机交互方式。

Method: 构建硬件系统实时流式传输人类的头戴摄像头视角、目光轨迹和语音，语音转文字后作为语言指令。设计分层策略：高层策略根据多模态信号推断人类意图，生成细粒度指令+关键帧（如指向目标物体的画面）组成的子任务序列；低层策略执行这些子任务。

Result: 在人类-机器人交互任务中，EDITH使机器人能够在人类仅通过简短的非语言信号表达意图时也能执行相应动作，相比仅使用语言指令，显著减少了用户传达意图所需付出的努力。

Conclusion: EDITH证明了融合非语言信号（第一人称视角、目光）与语言指令的机器人框架能够实现更自然、更高效的人机交互，为未来机器人理解人类隐含意图提供了可行方案。

Abstract: For natural human-robot interaction, a robot must understand human intent expressed not only through language but also through nonverbal signals such as gestures and gaze. However, current robot policies rely on language instructions as the sole interface for conveying intent, leaving nonverbal signals unused and placing the full burden of communication. In this work, we present EDITH, a robot framework that captures the human's nonverbal signals through continuous streams of first-person view and gaze from smart glasses, and uses them alongside language instructions as inputs to the robot policy. Our hardware system streams the human's first-person view, gaze, and speech to the robot in real time, transcribing the speech into language instructions. To handle these rich but noisy signals, we design a hierarchical policy in which a high-level policy infers the human's intent and produces a sequence of subtasks, where each subtask is represented as a fine-grained instruction paired with a keyframe that grounds the intent in the scene (e.g., the frame where the human points at the target object). A low-level policy then executes these subtasks. In our experiments on human-robot interactive tasks, EDITH enables the robot to act on the human's nonverbal signals even when intent is expressed only briefly, and significantly reduces user effort to convey intent compared to using language instructions alone. Visit our project page for source code and real-robot demo videos.

</details>


### [167] [MARCH: Model-Assisted Reinforcement Learning for the Perceptive Control of Humanoids over Sparse Footholds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10288)
*Codrin Crismariu,Ryan K. Cosner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种模型辅助强化学习框架，结合模型驱动与模型自由方法的优势，通过三阶段训练（简化模型生成安全轨迹→CLF奖励指导教师策略→蒸馏为视觉学生策略），使双足机器人在稀疏地形上实现稳健、平滑且安全的行走。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏地形上的双足行走面临两难：基于模型的方法精确但脆弱，对不确定性敏感；无模型方法鲁棒但难以发现安全关键运动所需的精确约束动作。小误差可能导致灾难性失败，需要融合两者优势。

Method: 三阶段模型辅助强化学习：1) 使用简化模型生成安全参考轨迹；2) 基于控制李雅普诺夫函数(CLF)构建奖励，训练特权教师策略；3) 将教师策略蒸馏为基于视觉的学生策略。

Result: 该框架提升了样本效率，减少了复杂学习课程的需求，实现了更平滑的运动行为，在踏脚石性能上与无模型基线相当。在仿真和Unitree G1人形机器人实物上均验证成功。

Conclusion: 模型辅助方法有效结合了模型驱动的精确性与强化学习的鲁棒性，为稀疏地形上的双足 locomotion 提供了一种实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Perceptive bipedal locomotion over sparse terrain remains a difficult challenge: model-based methods are precise but brittle to uncertainty, while model-free methods are robust but struggle to discover the precise, constrained motions required for safety-critical locomotion where small errors can cause catastrophic failures. We propose a model-assisted reinforcement learning (RL) framework that combines both perspectives in three steps: (1) generate a safe reference trajectory using simplified models; (2) train a privileged teacher policy guided by a control Lyapunov function (CLF) reward built around the safe reference trajectory; and (3) distill the teacher into a vision-based student policy. We show that this model-assistance procedure produces physically grounded locomotion, improving sample efficiency, reducing the need for a complex learning curriculum, and achieving smoother locomotion behavior alongside stepping stone performance comparable to model-free baselines. We validate our approach in simulation and demonstrate successful deployment on a Unitree G1 humanoid robot navigating sparse footholds with lateral constraints.

</details>


### [168] [Improved Representation of Matrix Lie Group Operations through Tensor Notation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10289)
*Clark Taylor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将张量与爱因斯坦求和符号引入矩阵李群运算，通过更明晰的数学符号简化李导数计算，从而提升基于梯度的估计框架的可操作性与清晰度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献已证明李群在估计问题中的有效性，但矩阵李群相关的导数与运算在传统符号下表述复杂、不够直观，亟需一种更清晰的数学表示方法来降低使用门槛。

Method: 将张量理论和爱因斯坦求和约定应用于矩阵李群，重新表述和计算矩阵李导数，使李群中的微分运算与操作更加简洁、系统化。

Result: 提出了一种新颖但非全新能力的数学符号体系，该符号显著简化了在梯度估计框架中处理矩阵李群所需的导数与运算表达。

Conclusion: 本文核心贡献不在于新增功能，而在于提供一种更明晰的数学符号，有助于研究者更高效地理解和应用矩阵李群于估计问题中。

Abstract: Several recent papers have demonstrated the utility of using Lie groups within estimation problems, yielding improved accuracy and consistency. This paper introduces a new tool for describing operations with matrix Lie groups: tensors and the Einstein summation notation. While tensors and Einstein notation are well-known in other research fields, applying this mathematical notation to represent and compute matrix Lie derivatives is novel. More importantly, this new notation greatly clarifies the derivatives and operations necessary to work with matrix Lie Groups in (gradient-based) estimation frameworks. Therefore, the main contribution of this paper is not a new capability, but a more perspicuous mathematical notation for working with matrix Lie groups.

</details>


### [169] [SARM2: Multi-Task Stage Aware Reward Modeling for Self Improving Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10305)
*Qianzhong Chen,Hau Zheng,Justin Yu,Suning Huang,Jiankai Sun,Ken Goldberg,Chuan Wen,Pieter Abbeel,Yide Shentu,Philipp Wu,Mac Schwager*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RM（多任务阶段感知奖励模型）和SPIRAL（策略自改进框架），通过结合动作基元阶段估计器与MMoE价值头生成密集逐步奖励，在10个任务上使价值估计MSE降低80%，并将折叠短裤成功率从58%提升至100%、清洁白板从50%提升至90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略微调依赖行为克隆，需要昂贵的高质量演示；奖励模型可降低对演示的依赖，但现有方法要么需逐任务标注（精确但泛化差），要么太粗糙（VLM奖励模型无法捕捉细粒度长程进展），缺乏既密集准确又通用的奖励模型。

Method: 提出RM奖励模型：结合动作基元阶段估计器（Action-Primitive Stage Estimator）与多门控混合专家（MMoE）价值头，生成跨任务的密集逐步骤奖励；在此基础上提出SPIRAL框架，利用廉价自主rollout数据进行在策略奖励引导的VLA策略改进。

Result: 在10任务基准上，RM的价值估计MSE比最强基线降低80%；SPIRAL将折叠短裤成功率从58%提升至100%，清洁白板从50%提升至90%，证明了高质量密集奖励对稳定机器人数据飞轮的关键作用。

Conclusion: 高质量密集奖励是实现稳定机器人数据飞轮的关键，RM+SPIRAL有效降低了VLA策略对高质量演示的依赖，实现了接近完美的任务成功率。

Abstract: Fine-tuning vision-language-action (VLA) policies for long-horizon manipulation still relies heavily on behavior cloning, which requires costly high-quality demonstrations and keeps policies near the demonstration distribution. Reward models can reduce this dependence by reweighting demonstrations and providing dense supervision for on-robot reinforcement learning (RL), but they must be dense, accurate, and general. Existing methods fall short: task-specific stage-aware models are accurate but require per-task annotations, while general vision-language-model (VLM) reward models are broadly applicable but too coarse for fine-grained long-horizon progress. We introduce RM, a multi-task stage-aware reward model that combines an action-primitive-based stage estimator with a multi-gate Mixture-of-Experts (MMoE) value head to produce dense per-step rewards across manipulation tasks. Building on RM, we further propose SPIRAL (Self-Policy Improvement via Reward-Aligned Learning), an on-policy reward-guided framework that improves VLA policies from cheap autonomous rollouts. On a 10-task benchmark, RM reduces value-estimation MSE by 80% over the strongest baselines; when used in SPIRAL, it improves task success from around 50% to near-perfect performance on Folding Shorts (58% to 100%) and Cleaning Whiteboard (50% to 90%), showing that high-quality dense rewards are key to a stable robot data flywheel. Project website: https://qianzhong-chen.github.io/sarm2.github.io/.

</details>


### [170] [OMG: Omni-Modal Motion Generation for Generalist Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10340)
*Siqiao Huang,Kun-Ying Lee,Dongming Qiao,Guanqi He,Zhenyu Wang,Yitang Li,Shaoting Zhu,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OMG提出了一种基于扩散模型的全身控制框架，通过数据筛选和 multimodal 条件生成，实现了类人机器人的通用全身控制，展示了SOTA性能和良好的扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类人全身控制方法局限于少数技能策略（依赖繁重的奖励工程）或运动追踪器（难以扩展至新输入模态），缺乏通用性。作者认为构建通用类人控制的关键在于模仿生物运动系统的层级结构，打造一个可扩展的"大脑"。

Method: 提出了OMG框架，包含：(1) 精细的数据筛选、过滤和标注流程；(2) 基于扩散模型的运动生成主干网络，能够以语言、音频和人类参考动作作为条件进行多模态条件生成。

Result: OMG在全模态全身控制任务上达到了SOTA性能，展现出良好的模型扩展行为，并能高效适应新的数据分布和模态。

Conclusion: OMG是实现人形机器人基础模型的重要一步，验证了通过层级式架构设计（可扩展的"大脑"+反应式运动追踪"小脑"）实现通用全身控制的可行性。

Abstract: Humanoid whole-body control has made significant progress in recent years, yet existing approaches remain limited to few-skill policies with heavy reward engineering, or motion trackers that are difficult to extend to new input modalities. We argue that the key to general-purpose humanoid control is to build a scalable brain, a module capable of reasoning with diverse conditioning modalities, atop a reactive motion tracking cerebellum, mirroring the hierarchical structure of biological motor systems. Two challenges arise in realizing this vision: acquiring a vast amount of high-quality data to achieve general purpose control, and equipping the generator with the capability to condition on compositional, extensible multi-modal inputs. We present OMG, which addresses these challenges with a meticulous data curation, filtering and labeling pipeline, as well as a diffusion-based motion generation backbone that conditions on language, audio, and human reference motions. Extensive experiments validate OMG as an omni-modal whole-body controller exhibiting state-of-the-art performance, model scaling behavior and efficient adaptation to new distributions and modalities, marking a concrete step toward foundation models for humanoid robots.

</details>


### [171] [Rethinking Embodied Navigation via Relational Inductive Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10348)
*Weitao An,Chenghao Xu,Xu Yang,Cheng Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DB-Nav通过双重关系偏置（激活偏置与抑制偏置）重塑搜索空间，在无需昂贵在线VLM推理的情况下，显著提升了物体导航的成功率和路径效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物体导航方法依赖开放词汇检测器或VLM来回答"去哪里搜索"，但忽略了"哪些语义线索不可信"——即开放词汇感知容易受到假阳性、过时静态先验以及缺乏具身体验证导致的重复失败探索等系统性误导证据的污染，这些错误源于真实场景中结构化的物体关系。

Method: 提出DB-Nav框架，将目标中心关系分解为激活偏置（传播上下文证据）和抑制偏置（通过感知混淆和行为级证伪抑制不可靠区域）。两种偏置统一整合到关系激活-抑制探索图中，利用在线观测和失败访问来调节前沿探索价值。

Result: 在ObjectNav基准测试中，DB-Nav在成功率（SR）和路径长度加权成功率（SPL）上显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: DB-Nav提供了一个轻量级、可解释且鲁棒的导航框架，无需昂贵的在线VLM推理。

Abstract: Object navigation requires an agent to locate a target in an unknown environment through visual observations. Existing methods typically rely on open-vocabulary detectors or vision-language models (VLMs) to answer where to search, but often overlook what not to trust - which semantic cues are unreliable. Open-vocabulary perception is prone to systematic misleading evidence: false positives, outdated static priors, and repeated failed exploration due to lack of embodied verification, which contaminates mapping and decision-making. Such errors are rooted in structured object relations in real-world scenes. To address this, we propose DB-Nav, a framework that reshapes the search space via dual relational biases. It factorizes target-centric relations into an Activation Bias (propagates contextual evidence) and an Inhibition Bias (suppresses unreliable regions via perceptual confusion and action-level falsification). These biases are unified into a Relational Activation-Inhibition Exploration Graph that modulates frontier exploration values using online observations and failed accesses. Experiments on ObjectNav benchmarks show that DB-Nav significantly outperforms existing methods in success rate (SR) and Success weighted by Path Length (SPL), offering a lightweight, interpretable, and robust navigation framework without costly online VLM reasoning.

</details>


### [172] [HiMem-WAM: Hierarchical Memory-Gated World Action Models for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10363)
*Xiaoquan Sun,Ruijian Zhang,Chen Cao,Yihan Sun,Jiahui Chen,Zetian Xu,Bo Chen,Haijier Chen,Zhen Yang,Jiarun Zhu,Yijun Hong,JingZhe Xu,Jingrui Pang,Mingqi Yuan,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HiMem-WAM通过层级记忆门控机制（运动潜变量+技能潜变量+边界触发记忆更新）解决了世界动作模型在长程操作任务中的任务相关记忆问题，在多个基准测试中提升了鲁棒性和长期操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）在长程机器人操作中缺乏任务相关的记忆能力，难以处理需要时序依赖的长视野任务。

Method: 提出层级潜动作框架：联合学习低层运动潜变量和高层技能潜变量，实现结构化时序抽象；设计边界感知记忆门控机制：在预测的技能转换边界写入紧凑任务状态，无需测试时生成未来视频或光流估计。

Result: 在LIBERO、LIBERO-PLUS、RMBench及真实世界任务上评估，层级潜变量提升了部署扰动下的鲁棒性，记忆模块显著提升了依赖记忆的长程操作性能。

Conclusion: 层级记忆门控机制有效弥补了WAMs在长程任务中的记忆缺失，为具身智能的长期时序推理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) have emerged as a new powerful paradigm for embodied intelligence, learning action-relevant visual dynamics that significantly enhance generalization and robustness. However, existing WAMs still struggle with task-relevant memory in long-horizon robotic manipulation. To address this, we present HiMem-WAM, a Hierarchical Memory-Gated WAM that integrates motion-centric latent actions, high-level skill latents, and boundary-triggered memory updates. Specifically, we develop a hierarchical latent action framework that jointly learns low-level motion and high-level skill latents, providing structured temporal abstraction. Meanwhile, a boundary-aware memory gate writes compact task states at predicted skill transitions, enabling causal inference without test-time generation of future video or optical flow estimation. Evaluated on LIBERO, LIBERO-PLUS, RMBench and real-world tasks, HiMem-WAM shows that hierarchical latents improve robustness under deployment perturbations, and the memory module substantially benefits memory-dependent long-horizon manipulation.

</details>


### [173] [A Practical Recipe Towards Improving Sim-and-Real Correlation for VLA Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10366)
*Shuo Wang,Hanyuan Xu,Yingdong Hu,Fanqi Lin,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了仿真环境与真实世界在评估视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略时的一致性，提出了一套衡量仿真有用性的统一框架，并分析了何时以及如何有效利用仿真进行策略改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管仿真基准在真实性和多样性上取得了进展，但尚未被广泛接受为真实世界策略评估的可靠代理。作者希望探究仿真与真实世界评估之间的相关性，明确现有仿真器的局限性。

Method: 在多个仿真平台、VLA策略、任务和扰动因素上系统测量仿真与真实评估的一致性，包括策略排名一致性、性能相关性以及扰动维度的失败模式对比。

Result: 揭示了现有仿真器的局限性，识别出哪些仿真信号更接近真实部署表现，并给出了基于仿真进行策略微调的有效条件及后训练数据量对sim-to-real一致性的影响。

Conclusion: 为仿真设计者和使用仿真进行策略开发的从业者提供了指导，明确了提升仿真在VLA策略开发中有用性的方向。

Abstract: Simulation has become an essential tool for evaluating and improving vision-language-action (VLA) policies, offering scalable, reproducible, and controllable alternatives to costly real-world robot evaluation. Recent simulation benchmarks have made substantial progress on realism and diversity, yet these platforms have not been widely adopted as reliable proxies for real-world policy evaluation. In this work, we investigate this issue through the lens of sim-and-real correlation. We conduct a systematic study across multiple simulation platforms, VLA policies, tasks, and perturbation factors, measuring whether simulated evaluation preserves real-world conclusions in terms of policy ranking consistency, performance correlation, and perturbation-wise failure patterns. This analysis allows us to characterize the limitations of existing simulators and identify what kinds of simulation signals are more aligned with real-world deployment. We further examine how users should exploit simulation for policy improvement, including when simulator-based finetuning is beneficial and how the amount of post-training data affects sim-and-real alignment. Overall, our work provides a unified framework for measuring, interpreting, and improving the usefulness of simulation for VLA policies, offering guidance both for simulator designers and for practitioners who use simulation as part of the policy development pipeline.

</details>


### [174] [Test-time Adversarial Takeover: A Real-time Hijacking Interface against Robotic Diffusion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10371)
*Zi Yin,Peilin Chai,Siyuan Huang,Zhanhao Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为TAKO（测试时对抗性接管）的新型攻击方法，通过在视觉条件化路径上施加可重复使用的通用补丁，使攻击者能够实时操控冻结的机器人扩散策略，在多种任务和模型架构上实现了100%的接管成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗攻击主要针对视觉条件化动作生成策略的扰动层面（降低任务成功率或引发异常行为），而作者希望研究一种更强威胁——攻击者是否能实时接管并操控机器人策略，使其成为远程操纵的工具。

Method: TAKO通过可微扩散推理学习一个由少量可重复使用的通用对抗补丁组成的"词汇表"；测试时，攻击者在相机流中切换这些补丁，从而组合出攻击者选择的轨迹。该方法利用视觉条件化路径上的偏置能通过迭代生成推理持续存在的特性。

Result: 在4个任务（2D操作、模拟空中配送、模拟地面导航、物理世界地面导航）、2种视觉编码器（ResNet-18和EfficientNet-B0+Transformer）和3种生成推理家族（DDPM、DDIM和流匹配）上，人类操作员在所有评估设置中均实现了100%的接管成功率。

Conclusion: TAKO揭示了扩散式视觉运动策略存在严重安全漏洞：攻击者可通过视觉条件化路径实现完全接管，而自然的目标策略匹配基线方法因无法可靠地监督分布外目标偏移而失败，表明现有防御机制对此类威胁缺乏准备。

Abstract: Diffusion-based action generation has become a foundational component of embodied AI, but its reliance on visual conditioning leaves deployed visuomotor policies vulnerable to adversarial manipulation. Most prior attacks focus on disruption: they perturb the observation stream to reduce task success or induce erratic behavior. We study a stronger threat, Test-time Adversarial Takeover (TAKO), in which an attacker obtains a real-time steering interface over a frozen robot policy and turns it into a remotely piloted instrument. TAKO learns a small vocabulary of reusable universal patches through differentiable diffusion inference; at test time, the attacker switches among these patches in the camera stream to compose attacker-chosen trajectories. This works because the perturbation acts on the visual conditioning pathway, where the induced bias can persist through iterative generative inference. We further show that the natural targeted baseline, target-policy matching, fails because the victim policy cannot reliably supervise itself on out-of-distribution target shifts. Across four tasks (2D manipulation, simulated aerial delivery, simulated ground navigation, and physical-world ground navigation), two visual encoders (ResNet-18 and EfficientNet-B0 + Transformer), and three generative inference families (DDPM, DDIM, and flow matching), human operators achieve 100\% takeover success on attacker-defined objectives in every evaluated setting. The project page is available at https://tako-attack.github.io.

</details>


### [175] [UMI-Bench 1.0: An Open and Reproducible Real-World Benchmark for Tabletop Robotic Manipulation with UMI Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10382)
*Shi Jin,Yuntian Wang,Yuhui Duan,Di Wu,Gaoqi Dong,Xiaohang Liu,Xiaotong Li,Hongfei Jia,Zehao Zhang,Tianyu Wang,Zhongjie Jia,Yuanqi Yao,Chenjia Bai,Zhaxizhuoma,Siao Liu,Nieqing Cao,Jin Wang,Chao Yu,Yan Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UMI-Bench 1.0是一个专为UMI风格操作策略设计的本地化真实机器人基准测试平台，实现了从数据收集到部署评估的全流程标准化与可复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的真实世界基准测试并未专门针对UMI风格策略的"数据-部署"闭环设计，而这类策略的性能高度依赖腕部视觉观测、动作表示、数据采集和物理部署之间的耦合关系，因此需要一个专用的标准化评估平台。

Method: UMI-Bench采用本地优先的真实机器人评估框架，将数据收集、场景重置、策略执行、结果记录和任务因子分析统一到一个标准化协议中，确保整个评估流程可复现、可审计。

Result: 作为首个专门用于UMI操作模型真实世界评估的基准测试平台，UMI-Bench提供了衡量UMI训练策略在真实物理操作中泛化能力的实用测试环境。

Conclusion: UMI-Bench为理解UMI风格操作策略在实际部署中的可靠性提供了标准化评估工具，填补了该领域缺乏专用基准测试的空白。

Abstract: Real-robot evaluation is essential for understanding whether learned manipulation policies can operate reliably outside curated demonstrations. This need is particularly pressing for Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI)-style policies, whose performance depends on the coupling between wrist-view observations, action representation, data collection, and physical deployment. Existing real-world benchmarks have made important progress, but they are not designed around this UMI data-to-deployment setting. We present UMI-Bench 1.0, a local-first real-robot benchmark for standardized evaluation of UMI-style manipulation policies. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first benchmark dedicated to real-world evaluation of UMI-based manipulation models. UMI-Bench aligns data collection, scene reset, policy execution, result logging, and task-factor analysis within a unified protocol. By making the full evaluation process reproducible and auditable, UMI-Bench provides a practical testbed for measuring how UMI-trained policies generalize to real physical manipulation.

</details>


### [176] [Information-Preserving Continuous Occupancy Mapping with Variance-Weighted Submap Joining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10442)
*Zhuhua Bai,Yingyu Wang,Liang Zhao,Shoudong Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出首个连续概率子图拼接框架，在潜在log-odds空间中联合优化子图位姿和全局占用场，利用信息保持的稀疏贝叶斯公式将原始观测压缩为充分统计量，实现闭合形式的预测均值和方差估计，在仿真和真实大规模数据集上相比现有方法显著提升位姿精度和全局一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有占据栅格子图拼接方法在离散网格上操作，导致优化过程中梯度不平滑且忽略占据估计的不确定性。

Method: 提出连续概率子图拼接框架，在log-odds潜在空间中联合优化子图位姿与全局占用场，采用信息保持的稀疏贝叶斯压缩观测，推导闭合形式的预测均值和方差，支持解析雅可比优化。

Result: 在仿真和真实大规模数据集上，相比现有基于网格的子图拼接方法，实现了更高的位姿精度和更好的全局一致性，且地图表示更紧凑、不确定性估计更校准。

Conclusion: 提出的连续概率框架有效解决了离散网格方法在优化平滑性和不确定性建模方面的不足，为大规模SLAM子图融合提供了更准确且不确定性感知的解决方案。

Abstract: Large-scale SLAM remains challenging due to accumulated trajectory drift and the increasing computational cost of maintaining global consistency. Submap joining alleviates these issues by constructing locally consistent submaps and subsequently fusing them into a global map. However, existing occupancy-based submap joining methods operate on discrete grids, resulting in non-smooth gradients during optimization and neglecting the uncertainty associated with occupancy estimates. We propose the first continuous probabilistic submap joining framework that jointly optimizes submap poses and a global occupancy field in the latent log-odds space. The framework employs an information-preserving sparse Bayesian formulation that compresses raw occupancy observations into sufficient-statistic log-odds tuples while retaining the posterior information of the original observations. This yields closed-form predictive mean and variance estimates for occupancy mapping, which directly enable a submap joining formulation with analytical Jacobians, leading to more accurate submap joining and yielding a closed-form optimal global map upon pose convergence. Experiments on both simulated and large-scale real-world datasets demonstrate that the proposed method achieves higher pose accuracy and improved global consistency than state-of-the-art grid-based submap joining approaches, while producing more compact map representations and better-calibrated uncertainty estimates than existing continuous occupancy mapping methods.

</details>


### [177] [GuideWalk: Learning Unified Autonomous Navigation and Locomotion for Humanoid Robots across Versatile Terrains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10449)
*Haoxuan Han,Chen Chen,Linao Gong,Xin Yang,Hao Hu,Junhong Guo,Zhicheng He,Yao Su,Fenghua He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GuideWalk是一个统一的端到端框架，通过将可通行性感知导航指导与地形自适应 locomotion 教师相结合，实现了双足机器人在多样化地形上的稳定导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双足机器人虽已具备强大运动能力，但在多样化地形上的可靠导航仍有挑战，因为避障必须与动态可行的运动相协调。

Method: 提出导航模块提供显式速度引导以解耦避障与地形条件；提出复合教师蒸馏方案聚合目标导向指令和动态一致动作；通过强化学习和辅助行为克隆目标精炼蒸馏策略。

Result: 实验表明GuideWalk能够在保持稳定双足运动的同时实现稳定有效的导航。

Conclusion: 该统一框架有效解决了双足机器人在复杂地形上的导航难题，兼顾了运动稳定性与导航效率。

Abstract: Humanoid robots have achieved strong locomotion capabilities, but reliable navigation on versatile terrains remains challenging because obstacle avoidance must be coordinated with dynamically feasible motion. In this work, we present GuideWalk, a unified end-to-end framework that integrates traversability-aware navigation guidance with terrain-adaptive locomotion teacher for humanoid navigation. Specifically, we introduce a navigation module that provides explicit velocity guidance, decoupling obstacle avoidance from terrain conditions to enable robust planning across diverse environments. We propose a composite teacher distillation scheme, where goal-directed commands and dynamically consistent actions are aggregated and distilled into a single policy. To further improve robustness, the distilled policy is refined with reinforcement learning and an auxiliary behavior cloning objective, which promotes exploration while preserving desirable teacher behaviors. Experiments demonstrate that GuideWalk achieves stable and effective navigation while maintaining stable humanoid locomotion.

</details>


### [178] [Act on What You See: Unlocking Safe Social Navigation in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10495)
*Qingzi Wang,Xiyang Wu,Guangyao Shi,Dianwei Chen,Xianfeng Yang,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SALSA通过两阶段后训练框架，让预训练的VLA模型更好地利用其内部已有的社交信号，实现安全社交导航，近碰撞减少86.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练的VLA模型内部已编码行人-物体区分和未来碰撞信号，但行为克隆无法将这些信号转化为社交适当的动作，存在表征与动作生成之间的错配。

Method: 提出SALSA两阶段无标注后训练框架：(1) 社交行为对齐——将中间层社交特征桥接到动作头，并在反事实的人-物场景对上训练以打破视觉显著性捷径；(2) 时间安全对齐——提供自动生成的未来风险监督，实现预期性碰撞避免。

Result: 在SCAND和真实部署中，近碰撞减少86.4%，社交反事实准确率从53%提升至93%。

Conclusion: 更安全的社会导航可以通过教VLA策略利用它们已拥有的表征来实现，关键在于更好地对齐潜在表征与动作生成。

Abstract: Safe social navigation requires robots to distinguish people from ordinary obstacles and to react before danger becomes imminent. We show that pretrained Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models already encode pedestrian-object distinctions and future collision signals in their internal representations, but behavior cloning fails to translate these signals into socially appropriate actions. To address this mismatch, we propose SALSA, a two-stage annotation-free post-training framework: (1) social behavioral alignment bridges intermediate-layer social features to the action head and trains on counterfactual human-object scene pairs to break visual saliency shortcuts; (2) temporal safety alignment provides automatically generated future-risk supervision to enable anticipatory collision avoidance. On SCAND and real-world deployment, SALSA reduces near-collisions by 86.4% and improves social counterfactual accuracy from 53% to 93%, demonstrating that safer social navigation can be achieved by teaching VLA policies to act on representations they already possess. These results show that pretrained VLA policies can be adapted for safer social navigation by better aligning their latent representations with action generation.

</details>


### [179] [Uncovering Vulnerability of Vision-Language-Action Models under Joint-Level Physical Faults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10501)
*Minsoo Jo,Taeju Kwon,Junha Chun,Youngjoon Jeong,Taesup Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究机器人关节级物理故障对VLA模型的影响，提出轻量级残差校准框架J-PARC，通过推断关节故障状态进行自适应动作校正，在保持正常性能的同时提升故障鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型研究主要关注语义和感知层面的鲁棒性，忽略了机器人关节故障（如执行器退化、磨损摩擦等）导致动作-运动映射关系改变的关键问题，而这类故障在实际部署中普遍存在且影响巨大。

Method: 提出J-PARC（Joint-level Physical-fault Aware Residual Calibrator），在冻结VLA策略之上添加轻量级残差校准模块：首先从近期关节动力学中推断潜在关节故障状态，然后基于该状态条件化共享残差校准器，实现对故障关节的自适应动作修正。

Result: 实验表明J-PARC在关节级故障下显著提升了鲁棒性，同时保持了无故障环境下的性能不退化。

Conclusion: 关节级物理故障对VLA模型性能有显著的异构影响，不能仅归因于物理不可行性；轻量级残差校准框架是提升VLA系统在真实机器人部署中鲁棒性的有效途径。

Abstract: Deploying Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models in real robotic systems requires robustness not only to semantic and perceptual variations, but also to embodiment-side faults that change how actions are physically realized. Real robots can experience joint-level changes caused by actuator degradation, hardware faults, safety limits, collision damage, or wear-induced friction. These faults are critical because they alter the action-to-motion interface of a policy, disrupting the learned closed-loop relationship between commanded actions, realized motion, and subsequent observations. In this work, we study realistic joint-level physical faults and show that VLA models are vulnerable when predicted actions are executed through a perturbed robot body. Our analysis reveals joint-dependent effects, with heterogeneous degradation in task success across affected joints. We also show that performance drops cannot be attributed solely to physical infeasibility, since feasible faults such as increased joint friction can still substantially reduce success rates and induce closed-loop execution mismatch. Motivated by these findings, we propose Joint-level Physical-fault Aware Residual Calibrator (J-PARC), a lightweight residual calibration framework built on top of a frozen VLA policy. J-PARC infers a latent joint-fault regime from recent joint dynamics and conditions a shared residual calibrator on this regime, enabling adaptive action correction across faulty joints. Experiments show that J-PARC improves robustness under joint-level faults while preserving fault-free environment performance.

</details>


### [180] [VeriSpace: Spatially Grounded Action Verification for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10568)
*Guiyu Zhao,Longteng Guo,Junyou Zhu,Jun Fu,Yanghong Mei,Bin Cao,Jie Jiang,Xingjian He,Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VeriSpace，一个3D感知的动作验证器，用于VLA系统的测试时动作选择。通过双路径3D注入场景编码和空间接地动作推理两个组件，有效区分候选动作的细微差异，提升机器人操作的决策可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在测试时仅依赖单次动作预测，微小误差就可能导致抓取失败、碰撞或任务推进错误。因此需要测试时验证机制来评估多个候选动作，但可靠的验证需要同时区分几何细微差异和评估动作对任务目标的实际进展。

Method: VeriSpace包含两个关键组件：1) 双路径3D注入场景编码：构建同时保留视觉语义和显式3D几何的场景表示；2) 空间接地动作推理：通过推理任务相关的空间关系、几何有效性和预期的目标进展来评估每个动作。

Result: 在公开基准和真实机器人操作任务上，VeriSpace一致优于底层VLA策略和已有的基于验证的方法，在分布内和分布外场景下均有显著提升。

Conclusion: VeriSpace通过3D感知的测试时验证机制，有效增强了VLA系统的决策可靠性，且与现有VLA策略完全兼容。
